    <script>
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
            var menuButton = document.getElementById('nav-menu-button');
            var menu = document.getElementById('nav-menu');

            if (!menuButton || !menu) {
                return;
            }

            function setMenuState(open) {
                menuButton.setAttribute('aria-expanded', open ? 'true' : 'false');
                menu.classList.toggle('opacity-100', open);
                menu.classList.toggle('visible', open);
                menu.classList.toggle('pointer-events-auto', open);
                menu.classList.toggle('opacity-0', !open);
                menu.classList.toggle('invisible', !open);
                menu.classList.toggle('pointer-events-none', !open);
            }

            menuButton.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
                event.stopPropagation();
                var isOpen = menuButton.getAttribute('aria-expanded') === 'true';
                setMenuState(!isOpen);
            });

            document.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
                if (!menu.contains(event.target) && !menuButton.contains(event.target)) {
                    setMenuState(false);
                }
            });

            document.addEventListener('keydown', function (event) {
                if (event.key === 'Escape') {
                    setMenuState(false);
                }
            });

            menu.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
                if (event.target.tagName === 'A') {
                    setMenuState(false);
                }
            });
        });
    </script>

        <script>
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
            var modal = document.getElementById('player-modal');
            var closeBtn = document.getElementById('close-player');
            var modalTitle = document.getElementById('modal-title');
            var player = videojs('stream-player');
            var channelSearchInput = document.getElementById('channel-search-home');
            var channelSearchClear = document.getElementById('channel-search-home-clear');
            var channelSearchEmpty = document.getElementById('channel-search-empty');

            function applyChannelSearch() {
                if (!channelSearchInput) {
                    return;
                }

                var query = (channelSearchInput.value || '').toLowerCase().trim();
                var categoryCards = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.category-card'));
                var totalVisible = 0;

                categoryCards.forEach(function (card) {
                    var items = Array.from(card.querySelectorAll('.channel-item'));
                    var visibleInCard = 0;

                    items.forEach(function (item) {
                        var text = ((item.dataset.title || '') + ' ' + (item.dataset.category || '')).toLowerCase();
                        var matches = query === '' || text.indexOf(query) !== -1;
                        item.classList.toggle('hidden', !matches);
                        if (matches) {
                            visibleInCard++;
                            totalVisible++;
                        }
                    });

                    card.classList.toggle('hidden', visibleInCard === 0);
                });

                if (channelSearchEmpty) {
                    channelSearchEmpty.classList.toggle('hidden', totalVisible !== 0);
                }
            }

            function openPlayer(channel) {
                if (!channel || !channel.url) {
                    return;
                }

                modalTitle.textContent = channel.name || 'Live Stream';
                modal.classList.remove('hidden');
                modal.classList.add('flex');

                player.src({
                    type: 'application/x-mpegURL',
                    src: channel.url
                });

                if (channel.poster) {
                    player.poster(channel.poster);
                }

                var playAttempt = player.play();
                if (playAttempt && typeof playAttempt.catch === 'function') {
                    playAttempt.catch(function () {});
                }
            }

            function closePlayer() {
                modal.classList.add('hidden');
                modal.classList.remove('flex');
                player.pause();
            }

            document.querySelectorAll('.channel-item').forEach(function (item) {
                item.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    var raw = item.getAttribute('data-channel') || '{}';
                    var channel = {};
                    try {
                        channel = JSON.parse(raw);
                    } catch (e) {
                        channel = {};
                    }

                    if (item.getAttribute('href')) {
                        window.history.pushState({}, '', item.getAttribute('href'));
                    }

                    openPlayer(channel);
                });
            });

            closeBtn.addEventListener('click', closePlayer);
            modal.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
                if (event.target === modal) {
                    closePlayer();
                }
            });

            if (channelSearchInput) {
                channelSearchInput.addEventListener('input', applyChannelSearch);
            }

            if (channelSearchClear) {
                channelSearchClear.addEventListener('click', function () {
                    if (!channelSearchInput) {
                        return;
                    }
                    channelSearchInput.value = '';
                    applyChannelSearch();
                    channelSearchInput.focus();
                });
            }

            applyChannelSearch();

                    });
    </script>
    </body>
</html>
